			OR = {
				western_liberals_are_in_power = yes
				neutrality_neutral_oligarch_are_in_power = yes
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus POL_koalicja_obywatelska"
			if = {
				limit = { western_liberals_are_in_power = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { add_col_one = 15 }
				add_coalition_members_effect = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { neutrality_neutral_oligarch_are_in_power = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { add_col_one = 2 }
				add_coalition_members_effect = yes

				limit = { has_idea = partial_draft_army }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = partial_draft_army
					add_idea = volunteer_army
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_idea = draft_army }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = draft_army
					add_idea = volunteer_army
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 150
		}
	}

			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = the_clergy }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
				change_the_clergy_opinion = yes
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = small_medium_business_owners
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = industrial_conglomerates
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = landowners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = landowners
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = farmers }
						swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = farmers
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
					}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = oligarchs }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = oligarchs
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = international_bankers }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = international_bankers
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = maritime_industry }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = maritime_industry
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = the_military }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = the_military
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
